def _resolve_nxt_ws_key(code):
    """NXT NS3 구독에는 LS가 반환한 거래소별 종목코드(ex_shcode)를 사용한다."""
    code = str(code or "").zfill(6)
    with NXT_WS_KEY_LOCK:
        cached = NXT_WS_KEY_CACHE.get(code)
        if cached:
            return cached

    engine = get_engine()
    ex_key = ""
    try:
        body = {"t8450InBlock": {"shcode": code, "exchgubun": "N"}}
        j = engine.ls._post_tr("/stock/market-data", "t8450", body).json()
        root = j.get("t8450OutBlock") or {}
        ex_key = str(root.get("ex_shcode") or "").strip()
    except Exception as e:
        logging.getLogger("alpha500_ws").debug("NXT WS KEY t8450 %s: %s", code, str(e)[:120])

    if not ex_key:
        try:
            now_hm = now_kst().strftime("%H%M%S")
            body = {"t8454InBlock": {
                "shcode": code, "cvolume": 0, "starttime": "", "endtime": now_hm,
                "cts_time": "", "exchgubun": "N",
            }}
            j = engine.ls._post_tr("/stock/market-data", "t8454", body).json()
            root = j.get("t8454OutBlock") or {}
            rows = j.get("t8454OutBlock1") or []
            ex_key = str(root.get("ex_shcode") or "").strip()
            if not ex_key:
                for row in rows[:5]:
                    ex_key = str((row or {}).get("ex_shcode") or "").strip()
                    if ex_key:
                        break
        except Exception as e:
            logging.getLogger("alpha500_ws").debug("NXT WS KEY t8454 %s: %s", code, str(e)[:120])

    if not ex_key:
        raise RuntimeError(f"NXT ex_shcode unavailable for {code}")
    with NXT_WS_KEY_LOCK:
        NXT_WS_KEY_CACHE[code] = ex_key
        NXT_WS_KEY_REVERSE[ex_key] = code
    logging.getLogger("alpha500_ws").info("NXT WS KEY resolved symbol=%s key=%s", code, ex_key)
    return ex_key


def _subscription_tr_key(venue, code):
    if str(venue or "").upper() == "NXT":
        return _resolve_nxt_ws_key(code)
    return str(code or "").zfill(6)


def set_progress(phase: str, current: int = 0, total: int = 0, message: str = ""):
    with LOCK:
        STATE["phase"] = phase
        STATE["progress_current"] = int(current or 0)
        STATE["progress_total"] = int(total or 0)
        STATE["progress_message"] = message or phase


class EngineProgressHandler(logging.Handler):
    """통합엔진 로그를 화면 진행률로 변환한다."""
    def emit(self, record):
        try:
            msg = record.getMessage()
            m = re.search(r"UNIVERSE HANDOFF source=(\S+) kept=(\d+) filled=(\d+) target=(\d+) venue=(\S+)", msg)
            if m:
                src,kept,filled,total,venue = m.groups(); set_progress("UNIVERSE", int(total), int(total), f"{venue} 공통500 인계 · 유지 {kept} · 보충 {filled}"); return
            m = re.search(r"runtime universe selected=(\d+).*venue=(\S+).*session=(\S+)", msg)
            if m:
                n,venue,session=m.groups(); set_progress("UNIVERSE", int(n), int(n), f"{session} · {venue} 유니버스 {n}개 완료"); return
            m = re.search(r"NXT QUOTES (\d+)/(\d+)", msg)
            if m:
                a,b=map(int,m.groups()); set_progress("NXT_QUOTES", a, b, f"NXT 현재가 조회 {a}/{b}"); return
            m = re.search(r"history bootstrap/refresh required=(\d+)", msg)
            if m:
                n = int(m.group(1)); set_progress("HISTORY", 0, n, f"과거봉 구축 0/{n}"); return
            m = re.search(r"history bootstrap (\d+)/(\d+)", msg)
            if m:
                a,b = map(int,m.groups()); set_progress("HISTORY", a, b, f"과거봉 구축 {a}/{b}"); return
            m = re.search(r"FAST STAGE1 (\d+)/(\d+)", msg)
            if m:
                a,b = map(int,m.groups()); set_progress("STAGE1", a, b, f"Alpha Heikin-Ashi 1차 {a}/{b}"); return
            m = re.search(r"pipeline (\d+) -> stage2=(\d+) -> deep=(\d+)", msg)
            if m:
                uni,s2,dp = map(int,m.groups()); set_progress("STAGE2", s2, max(s2,1), f"{STATE.get('venue','-')} {uni}종목 · Stage2 {s2}개 · Deep {dp}개"); return
            m = re.search(r"DEEP (\d+)/(\d+)", msg)
            if m:
                a,b = map(int,m.groups()); set_progress("DEEP", a, b, f"LS 딥스캔 {a}/{b}"); return
            if "finalizing" in msg.lower() or "FINALIZE start" in msg:
                set_progress("FINALIZING", 0, 1, "최종 후보·섹터 이동·히스토리 저장 중"); return
            if "FINALIZE done" in msg or "pipeline complete" in msg:
                set_progress("COMPLETE", 1, 1, "판정 완료"); return
        except Exception:
            pass


_progress_handler = EngineProgressHandler()
_progress_handler.setLevel(logging.INFO)
logging.getLogger("alpha500_integrated").addHandler(_progress_handler)


def load_latest_for_venue(venue):
    venue = str(venue or "KRX").upper()
    for p in (RESULT_DIR / f"latest_alpha500_{venue}.json", RESULT_DIR / "latest_alpha500.json"):
        try:
            data = json.loads(p.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            if str(data.get("venue") or "").upper() == venue:
                return data
        except (OSError, ValueError, AttributeError):
            continue
    # NXT는 20:00 이후/익일 첫 정상 판정 전까지 마지막 EOD HOLD를 보조 원천으로 유지한다.
    if venue == "NXT":
        try:
            data = _read_nxt_eod_hold()
            if isinstance(data, dict) and str(data.get("venue") or "").upper() == "NXT":
                return data.get("summary") if isinstance(data.get("summary"), dict) else data
        except Exception:
            pass
    return None


def load_latest():
    venue = str(market_session_now().get("venue") or "KRX").upper()
    return load_latest_for_venue(venue)


def get_engine():
    global ENGINE
    with ENGINE_LOCK:
        if ENGINE is None:
            ENGINE = Alpha500Integrated(MASTER)
        return ENGINE


def get_rest_history(venue):
    # Separate REST overlay provider avoids changing the running scan's venue.
    global REST_ENGINE
    with ENGINE_LOCK:
        if REST_ENGINE is None:
            from 오백종목_섹터에너지_알파하이킨아시_통합_V1_0 import FastHistory500
            REST_ENGINE = FastHistory500(get_engine().ls, db_path=str(RESULT_DIR.parent / "alpha500_live_bars.sqlite3"))
        REST_ENGINE.set_venue(venue, "N" if venue == "NXT" else "K")
        return REST_ENGINE

def _quote_pct(q):
    try:
        v = q.get("diff")
        return float(v) if v not in (None, "") else None
    except Exception:
        return None

def _quote_price(q):
    try:
        v = float(q.get("price") or 0)
        return v if v > 0 else None
    except Exception:
        return None

def _live_targets(summary):
    """화면에서 실시간으로 추적할 종목과 시장 구분을 반환한다."""
    if not isinstance(summary, dict):
        return {}
    targets = {}
    for key in ("live_pool", "top_final", "top_watch"):
        for r in summary.get(key) or []:
            c = str(r.get("symbol", "")).zfill(6)
            if c.isdigit() and len(c) == 6:
                targets.setdefault(c, str(r.get("market") or "").upper())
    for r in (summary.get("selection_history") or [])[:20]:
        c = str(r.get("symbol", "")).zfill(6)
        if c.isdigit() and len(c) == 6:
            targets.setdefault(c, str(r.get("market") or "").upper())
    limit = max(1, int(os.getenv("LIVE_QUOTE_MAX", "80")))
    return dict(list(targets.items())[:limit])


def _live_codes(summary):
    return list(_live_targets(summary).keys())


def _quote_is_fresh(code: str, max_age: int = WS_STALE_SECONDS) -> bool:
    with LIVE_LOCK:
        meta = dict(LIVE_QUOTE_META.get(code) or {})
    t = float(meta.get("recv_monotonic") or 0.0)
    return bool(t and (time.monotonic() - t) <= max_age)


def _quote_source(code: str) -> str:
    with LIVE_LOCK:
        return str((LIVE_QUOTE_META.get(code) or {}).get("source") or "")


def _set_live_quote(code: str, q: dict, source: str, venue: str = ""):
    global LIVE_UPDATED_KST
    if not code or not isinstance(q, dict):
        return
    q2 = dict(q)
    q2["symbol"] = code
    q2["_source"] = source
    q2["_updated_kst"] = now_kst_iso()
    with LIVE_LOCK:
        LIVE_QUOTES[code] = q2
        LIVE_QUOTE_META[code] = {
            "recv_monotonic": time.monotonic(),
            "updated_kst": q2["_updated_kst"],
            "source": source,
            "venue": str(venue or "").upper(),
        }
        LIVE_UPDATED_KST = q2["_updated_kst"]


def _flatten_dict(obj):
    out = {}
    if isinstance(obj, dict):
        for k, v in obj.items():
            if isinstance(v, dict):
                out.update(_flatten_dict(v))
            elif not isinstance(v, (list, tuple)):
                out[str(k)] = v
    return out


def _first_value(d, keys, default=None):
    for k in keys:
        v = d.get(k)
        if v not in (None, ""):
            return v
    return default


def _extract_ws_quote(payload):
    """S3_/K3_/NS3 실시간 체결 메시지를 공통 quote 형식으로 정규화한다."""
    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
        return None, None, None
    header = payload.get("header") if isinstance(payload.get("header"), dict) else {}
    body = payload.get("body") if isinstance(payload.get("body"), dict) else payload
    flat = _flatten_dict(body)
    tr_cd = str(header.get("tr_cd") or flat.get("tr_cd") or "").strip()
    code = str(_first_value(flat, ["shcode", "expcode", "symbol", "code", "isu_no"], "")).strip()
    if code.startswith("A") and len(code) == 7:
        code = code[1:]
    if not (code.isdigit() and len(code) == 6):
        tr_key = str(header.get("tr_key") or flat.get("ex_shcode") or "").strip()
        if tr_key.isdigit() and len(tr_key) == 6:
            code = tr_key
        else:
            with NXT_WS_KEY_LOCK:
                mapped = NXT_WS_KEY_REVERSE.get(tr_key)
            if mapped:
                code = mapped
    if not (code.isdigit() and len(code) == 6):
        return None, None, tr_cd
    price = _first_value(flat, ["price", "cheprice", "curpr", "close", "last"])
    diff = _first_value(flat, ["drate", "diff", "change_rate", "rate", "chg_rate"])
    change = _first_value(flat, ["change", "chg", "change_price"])
    volume = _first_value(flat, ["volume", "cvolume", "accvolume", "cum_volume"])
    value = _first_value(flat, ["value", "accvalue", "cum_value"])
    q = {"price": price, "diff": diff, "change": change, "volume": volume, "value": value}
    if _quote_price(q) is None:
        return None, None, tr_cd
    return code, q, tr_cd


def _ws_target_map(summary, venue):
    """판정 완료를 기다리지 않고 WS를 먼저 살리는 대상 집합을 만든다.

    1순위: 직전/현재 판정의 후보+히스토리. 세션이 바뀌어도 종목코드는 재사용하되
           현재 venue의 실시간 채널로 새로 구독한다.
    2순위: 결과 파일이 없거나 비어 있으면 LS 종목마스터에서 소수 probe 종목을 사용한다.
    """
    targets = _live_targets(summary) if isinstance(summary, dict) else {}
    if targets:
        return targets
    try:
        engine = get_engine()
        universe = engine.selector.get_ls_universe()
        if venue == "NXT":
            universe = [x for x in universe if bool(getattr(x, "nxt", False))]
        limit = max(5, min(30, int(os.getenv("WS_BOOTSTRAP_MAX", "20"))))
        return {x.code: str(x.market or "").upper() for x in universe[:limit]}
    except Exception as e:
        logging.getLogger("alpha500_ws").warning("WS bootstrap targets unavailable: %s", str(e)[:220])
        return {}


def _ws_subscriptions(summary, venue):
    targets = _ws_target_map(summary, venue)
    subs = []
    nxt_eligible = None
    if venue == "NXT":
        try:
            nxt_eligible = {str(x.code).zfill(6) for x in get_engine().selector.get_ls_universe() if bool(getattr(x, "nxt", False))}
        except Exception:
            nxt_eligible = set()
    for code, market in targets.items():
        if venue == "NXT":
            # 직전 KRX 후보라도 NXT 거래가능 종목만 NS3에 등록한다.
            if nxt_eligible is not None and code not in nxt_eligible:
                continue
            subs.append(("NS3", code))
        else:
            m = str(market or "").upper()
            if "KOSDAQ" in m or m in {"Q", "2"}:
                subs.append(("K3_", code))
            elif "KOSPI" in m or m in {"P", "1"}:
                subs.append(("S3_", code))
            else:
                # market 표기가 없으면 LS master에서 시장구분을 보강한다.
                try:
                    item = next((x for x in get_engine().selector.get_ls_universe() if x.code == code), None)
                    mm = str(getattr(item, "market", "") or "").upper()
                    if "KOSDAQ" in mm:
                        subs.append(("K3_", code))
                    elif "KOSPI" in mm:
                        subs.append(("S3_", code))
                except Exception:
                    continue
    if venue == "NXT":
        subs = subs[:max(1, int(os.getenv("NXT_WS_SUB_MAX", "20")))]
    else:
        subs = subs[:max(1, int(os.getenv("LIVE_QUOTE_MAX", "80")))]
    return subs


def _set_ws_state(status, subscribed=None, channels=None, error=None, last_message=False, rest_fallback=None):
    changed = False
    snapshot = None
    with LOCK:
        prev = (STATE.get("ws_status"), STATE.get("ws_subscribed"), tuple(STATE.get("ws_channels") or []), STATE.get("ws_error"))
        STATE["ws_status"] = status
        if subscribed is not None:
            STATE["ws_subscribed"] = int(subscribed)
        if channels is not None:
            STATE["ws_channels"] = list(channels)
        STATE["ws_error"] = error
        if last_message:
            STATE["ws_last_message_kst"] = now_kst_iso()
        if rest_fallback is not None:
            STATE["rest_fallback_active"] = bool(rest_fallback)
        cur = (STATE.get("ws_status"), STATE.get("ws_subscribed"), tuple(STATE.get("ws_channels") or []), STATE.get("ws_error"))
        changed = cur != prev
        snapshot = cur
    if changed:
        logging.getLogger("alpha500_ws").info(
            "WS STATE status=%s subscribed=%s channels=%s error=%s",
            snapshot[0], snapshot[1], list(snapshot[2]), snapshot[3] or ""
        )


def live_ws_loop():
    """LS WebSocket 실시간 시세. NXT는 ex_shcode + 회로차단으로 Broken pipe 폭주를 방지한다."""
    log = logging.getLogger("alpha500_ws")
    nxt_failures = 0
    circuit_until = 0.0
    while True:
        ws = None
        venue = "NXT"
        received_this_session = False
        try:
            if os.getenv("LIVE_WS_ENABLED", "true").strip().lower() not in {"1", "true", "yes", "y", "on"}:
                _set_ws_state("DISABLED", subscribed=0, channels=[], error=None)
                time.sleep(5)
                continue
            info = market_session_now()
            summary = load_latest()
            if not bool(info.get("scan_allowed")):
                _set_ws_state("WAITING_SESSION", subscribed=0, channels=[], error=None)
                time.sleep(2)
                continue
            venue = str(info.get("venue") or "NXT").upper()

            if venue == "NXT" and time.monotonic() < circuit_until:
                remain = max(1, int(circuit_until - time.monotonic()))
                _set_ws_state("REST_FALLBACK", subscribed=0, channels=["NS3"],
                              error=f"NXT WS circuit open {remain}s", rest_fallback=True)
                time.sleep(min(5, remain))
                continue

            subs = _ws_subscriptions(summary, venue)
            if not subs:
                _set_ws_state("WAITING_TARGETS", subscribed=0, channels=[], error=None)
                time.sleep(2)
                continue

            send_subs = []
            if venue == "NXT":
                for tr_cd, code in subs:
                    try:
                        tr_key = _subscription_tr_key(venue, code)
                        send_subs.append((tr_cd, code, tr_key))
                    except Exception as e:
                        log.warning("NXT WS KEY skip symbol=%s error=%s", code, str(e)[:160])
                if not send_subs:
                    _set_ws_state("REST_FALLBACK", subscribed=0, channels=["NS3"],
                                  error="NXT ex_shcode unavailable", rest_fallback=True)
                    time.sleep(REST_FALLBACK_SECONDS)
                    continue
            else:
                send_subs = [(tr_cd, code, str(code).zfill(6)) for tr_cd, code in subs]

            engine = get_engine()
            engine.ls._ensure_token()
            token = engine.ls.token
            with LOCK:
                STATE["ls_status"] = "CONNECTED"
                STATE["ws_transport_mode"] = "NXT_NS3_EX_SHCODE" if venue == "NXT" else "KRX_DIRECT_6DIGIT"
            log.info("LS CONNECTED token_ready=true")
            _set_ws_state("CONNECTING", subscribed=0, channels=[], error=None, rest_fallback=(venue == "NXT"))
            ws = websocket.create_connection(WS_URL, timeout=5, enable_multithread=True)
            ws.settimeout(2)
            channels = sorted(set(x[0] for x in send_subs))

            sent = 0
            gap = max(0.05, int(os.getenv(
                "NXT_WS_SUBSCRIBE_INTERVAL_MS" if venue == "NXT" else "WS_SUBSCRIBE_INTERVAL_MS",
                "350" if venue == "NXT" else "180"
            )) / 1000.0)
            for tr_cd, code, tr_key in send_subs:
                ws.send(json.dumps({
                    "header": {"token": token, "tr_type": "3"},
                    "body": {"tr_cd": tr_cd, "tr_key": tr_key},
                }, ensure_ascii=False))
                sent += 1
                with LOCK:
                    STATE["ws_last_key"] = tr_key
                if venue == "NXT":
                    time.sleep(gap)

            signature = (venue, tuple(subs))
            last_tick_monotonic = time.monotonic()
            last_ping_monotonic = 0.0
            _set_ws_state("SUBSCRIBED", subscribed=sent, channels=channels, error=None,
                          rest_fallback=(venue == "NXT"))
            log.info("WS CONNECTED venue=%s channels=%s subscribed=%s key_mode=%s",
                     venue, channels, sent, "EX_SHCODE" if venue == "NXT" else "6DIGIT")

            while True:
                info2 = market_session_now()
                summary2 = load_latest()
                venue2 = str(info2.get("venue") or venue).upper()
                subs2 = _ws_subscriptions(summary2, venue2) if bool(info2.get("scan_allowed")) else []
                if (venue2, tuple(subs2)) != signature:
                    break

                stale_limit = max(WS_STALE_SECONDS, int(os.getenv("NXT_WS_STALE_SECONDS", "45"))) if venue == "NXT" else WS_STALE_SECONDS
                tick_age = time.monotonic() - last_tick_monotonic
                if tick_age > stale_limit:
                    _set_ws_state("STALE", error=f"WS tick stale {int(tick_age)}s", rest_fallback=True)
                    # ping 성공만으로 실시간 구독 스트림이 정상이라고 보지 않는다.
                    # 실제 Tick이 stale_limit 동안 없으면 소켓을 닫고 재연결/재구독한다.
                    raise RuntimeError(f"WS tick stale {int(tick_age)}s; force reconnect")

                try:
                    raw = ws.recv()
                except websocket.WebSocketTimeoutException:
                    now_m = time.monotonic()
                    if now_m - last_ping_monotonic >= 10.0:
                        try:
                            ws.ping()
                            last_ping_monotonic = now_m
                        except Exception as e:
                            raise RuntimeError(f"LS WebSocket ping failed: {e}")
                    continue

                if raw in (None, ""):
                    raise RuntimeError("LS WebSocket closed")
                try:
                    payload = json.loads(raw)
                except Exception:
                    continue
                if market_session_now().get("venue") != venue:
                    break

                hdr = payload.get("header") if isinstance(payload, dict) and isinstance(payload.get("header"), dict) else {}
                rsp_cd = str(hdr.get("rsp_cd") or "").strip()
                rsp_msg = str(hdr.get("rsp_msg") or "").strip()
                if rsp_cd:
                    if rsp_cd in {"00000", "0"}:
                        with LOCK:
                            STATE["ws_ack_count"] = int(STATE.get("ws_ack_count") or 0) + 1
                    else:
                        with LOCK:
                            STATE["ws_reject_count"] = int(STATE.get("ws_reject_count") or 0) + 1
                        log.warning("WS subscription response rsp_cd=%s msg=%s", rsp_cd, rsp_msg[:160])

                code, q, tr_cd = _extract_ws_quote(payload)
                if code and q and (tr_cd, code) in subs:
                    received_this_session = True
                    nxt_failures = 0
                    last_tick_monotonic = time.monotonic()
                    _set_live_quote(code, q, f"WS:{tr_cd or 'REAL'}", venue)
                    with LOCK:
                        STATE["ws_tick_count"] = int(STATE.get("ws_tick_count") or 0) + 1
                        STATE["ws_last_tr"] = tr_cd or "REAL"
                        STATE["ws_last_symbol"] = code
                        STATE["ws_last_tick_kst"] = now_kst_iso()
                        STATE["ws_last_price"] = _quote_price(q)
                        tick_n = STATE["ws_tick_count"]
                    if tick_n <= 3 or tick_n % 100 == 0:
                        log.info("WS TICK count=%s tr=%s symbol=%s price=%s diff=%s",
                                 tick_n, tr_cd or "REAL", code, q.get("price"), q.get("diff"))
                    _set_ws_state("RECEIVING", last_message=True, rest_fallback=False)

        except Exception as e:
            with LOCK:
                STATE["ws_reconnect_count"] = int(STATE.get("ws_reconnect_count") or 0) + 1
            if venue == "NXT" and not received_this_session:
                nxt_failures += 1
            else:
                nxt_failures = 0
            _set_ws_state("STALE", error=str(e)[:240], rest_fallback=True)
            log.warning("WS reconnect venue=%s failures=%s error=%s", venue, nxt_failures, e)

            if venue == "NXT" and nxt_failures >= max(1, int(os.getenv("NXT_WS_MAX_CONSECUTIVE_FAILURES", "3"))):
                pause = max(15, int(os.getenv("NXT_WS_CIRCUIT_BREAK_SECONDS", "60")))
                circuit_until = time.monotonic() + pause
                log.warning("NXT WS CIRCUIT OPEN failures=%s pause=%ss; REST fallback continues", nxt_failures, pause)
                nxt_failures = 0
                time.sleep(min(5, pause))
            else:
                base = max(2, int(os.getenv("WS_RECONNECT_BACKOFF_SECONDS", "5")))
                max_b = max(base, int(os.getenv("NXT_WS_RECONNECT_MAX_SECONDS", "30")))
                attempt = max(0, nxt_failures - 1) if venue == "NXT" else 0
                backoff = min(max_b, base * (2 ** min(attempt, 3)))
                time.sleep(backoff)
        finally:
            if ws is not None:
                try:
                    ws.close()
                except Exception:
                    pass


def _apply_live_overlay(summary):
    if not isinstance(summary, dict):
        return summary
    out = json.loads(json.dumps(summary, ensure_ascii=False, default=str))
    with LIVE_LOCK:
        qmap = dict(LIVE_QUOTES)
        meta = {k: dict(v) for k, v in LIVE_QUOTE_META.items()}
        updated = LIVE_UPDATED_KST
    sources = set()
    for key in ("live_pool", "top_final", "top_watch", "selection_history"):
        for r in out.get(key) or []:
            if str(r.get("venue") or out.get("venue") or "").upper() != str(out.get("venue") or "").upper():
                continue
            c = str(r.get("symbol", "")).zfill(6)
            q = qmap.get(c)
            if not q:
                continue
            m = meta.get(c) or {}
            if str(m.get("venue") or out.get("venue") or "").upper() != str(out.get("venue") or "").upper():
                continue
            px = _quote_price(q)
            pct = _quote_pct(q)
            if px is not None:
                r["current_price"] = px
            if pct is not None:
                r["change_pct"] = pct
            src = str(m.get("source") or q.get("_source") or "LIVE")
            sources.add(src)
            r["live_quote"] = True
            r["live_source"] = src
            r["live_updated_kst"] = m.get("updated_kst")
    out["live_updated_kst"] = updated
    out["live_quote_count"] = len(qmap)
    out["live_price_source"] = ",".join(sorted(sources)) if sources else "WAITING"
    with LIVE_RANK_LOCK:
        snap = json.loads(json.dumps(LIVE_RANK_SNAPSHOT, ensure_ascii=False, default=str))
    same_venue = str(snap.get("venue") or "").upper() == str(out.get("venue") or "").upper()
    if same_venue and snap.get("live_deep30"):
        out["live_deep30"] = snap["live_deep30"]
    if same_venue and snap.get("live_watch10"):
        out["live_watch10"] = snap["live_watch10"]
    if same_venue and snap.get("live_rebalanced_kst"):
        out["live_rebalanced_kst"] = snap["live_rebalanced_kst"]

    with LIVE_SECTOR_LOCK:
        ss = json.loads(json.dumps(LIVE_SECTOR_SNAPSHOT, ensure_ascii=False, default=str))
    same_sector_venue = str(ss.get("venue") or "").upper() == str(out.get("venue") or "").upper()
    if same_sector_venue and ss.get("sector_energy"):
        out["sector_energy"] = ss["sector_energy"]
        out["top_clusters"] = ss.get("top_clusters") or ss["sector_energy"][:15]
        out["live_sector_updated_kst"] = ss.get("updated_kst")
        out["live_sector_source"] = ss.get("source") or "LIVE"
    with LOCK:
        out["ws_status"] = STATE.get("ws_status")
        out["ws_last_message_kst"] = STATE.get("ws_last_message_kst")
        out["ws_subscribed"] = STATE.get("ws_subscribed")
        out["ws_channels"] = STATE.get("ws_channels")
        out["rest_fallback_active"] = STATE.get("rest_fallback_active")
        out["live_pool_count"] = STATE.get("live_pool_count", 0)
        out["live_deep_count"] = STATE.get("live_deep_count", 0)
        out["live_watch_count"] = STATE.get("live_watch_count", 0)
        out["live_sector_updated_kst"] = out.get("live_sector_updated_kst") or STATE.get("live_sector_updated_kst")
        out["live_sector_count"] = STATE.get("live_sector_count", 0)
        out["live_sector_source"] = out.get("live_sector_source") or STATE.get("live_sector_source", "WAITING")
        out["live_sector_error"] = STATE.get("live_sector_error")
    return out



def _clamp_live(v, lo, hi):
    try:
        return max(lo, min(hi, float(v)))
    except Exception:
        return lo


def _live_proxy_score(row):
    integrated = float(row.get("integrated_score") or 0.0)
    energy = float(row.get("cluster_energy") or 0.0)
    mrs_score = float(row.get("market_rs_score") or 50.0)
    cp = float(row.get("current_price") or 0.0)
    ep = float(row.get("entry_price") or 0.0)
    ch = float(row.get("change_pct") or 0.0)
    er = ((cp / ep - 1.0) * 100.0) if cp > 0 and ep > 0 else 0.0

    momentum = 50.0 + 5.0 * _clamp_live(er, -5.0, 5.0) + 2.0 * _clamp_live(ch, -5.0, 5.0)
    if ch > 5.0:
        momentum -= min(20.0, 6.0 * (ch - 5.0))
    if er < -4.0:
        momentum -= 8.0
    momentum = _clamp_live(momentum, 0.0, 100.0)

    proxy = 0.55 * integrated + 0.15 * energy + 0.10 * mrs_score + 0.20 * momentum
    if str(row.get("cluster_state") or "") == "S4":
        proxy -= 6.0
    elif str(row.get("cluster_state") or "") == "S5":
        proxy -= 12.0
    return round(_clamp_live(proxy, 0.0, 100.0), 3), round(er, 3), round(momentum, 3)


def _seed_live_deep_cache(summary):
    now_mono = time.monotonic()
    venue = str(summary.get("venue") or "").upper()
    for r in (summary.get("live_pool") or []):
        code = str(r.get("symbol", "")).zfill(6)
        if not code.isdigit() or not r.get("deep_verified"):
            continue
        key = f"{venue}:{code}"
        # 실시간으로 갱신된 Deep 값을 10분 스캔의 오래된 seed가 덮어쓰지 않는다.
        if key not in LIVE_DEEP_CACHE:
            LIVE_DEEP_CACHE[key] = {
                "deep_score": r.get("deep_score"),
                "deep_grade": r.get("deep_grade"),
                "deep_coverage": r.get("deep_coverage"),
                "trade_strength": r.get("trade_strength"),
                "foreign_net": r.get("foreign_net"),
                "institution_net": r.get("institution_net"),
                "program_net": r.get("program_net"),
                "verified_kst": r.get("deep_verified_at_kst") or summary.get("timestamp_kst"),
            }
        LIVE_DEEP_REFRESHED_AT.setdefault(key, now_mono)


def _live_rows_with_quotes(summary):
    rows = json.loads(json.dumps(summary.get("live_pool") or [], ensure_ascii=False, default=str))
    with LIVE_LOCK:
        qmap = dict(LIVE_QUOTES)
        meta = {k: dict(v) for k, v in LIVE_QUOTE_META.items()}
    venue = str(summary.get("venue") or "").upper()
    for r in rows:
        code = str(r.get("symbol", "")).zfill(6)
        q = qmap.get(code)
        m = meta.get(code) or {}
        if q and str(m.get("venue") or venue).upper() == venue:
            px = _quote_price(q)
            pct = _quote_pct(q)
            if px is not None:
                r["current_price"] = px
            if pct is not None:
                r["change_pct"] = pct
            r["live_source"] = str(m.get("source") or q.get("_source") or "LIVE")
            r["live_updated_kst"] = m.get("updated_kst")
        cache_key = f"{venue}:{code}"
        cache = LIVE_DEEP_CACHE.get(cache_key)
        if cache:
            r.update(cache)
            r["deep_verified"] = True
        proxy, er, momentum = _live_proxy_score(r)
        deep_score = float(r.get("deep_score") or 0.0)
        live_score = (0.72 * proxy + 0.28 * deep_score) if r.get("deep_verified") and deep_score > 0 else (proxy - 2.0)
        r["live_proxy_score"] = proxy
        r["entry_return_pct"] = er
        r["live_momentum_score"] = momentum
        r["live_score"] = round(_clamp_live(live_score, 0.0, 100.0), 3)
    return rows


def _deep_refresh_one(row, venue):
    code = str(row.get("symbol", "")).zfill(6)
    if not code.isdigit():
        return False
    with LOCK:
        if STATE.get("running"):
            return False

    venue_key = str(venue or "").upper()
    cache_key = f"{venue_key}:{code}"
    ttl = max(60, int(os.getenv("LIVE_DEEP_TTL_SECONDS", "300")))
    last = float(LIVE_DEEP_REFRESHED_AT.get(cache_key) or 0.0)
    if cache_key in LIVE_DEEP_CACHE and time.monotonic() - last < ttl:
        return False
    if not LIVE_DEEP_REFRESH_LOCK.acquire(blocking=False):
        return False
    try:
        info = market_session_now()
        if not info.get("scan_allowed") or str(info.get("venue") or "").upper() != str(venue).upper():
            return False
        engine = get_engine()
        d = engine.deep.collect(code)
        d.sector_code = str(row.get("ENERGY_CLUSTER") or "")
        d.sector_breadth = float(row.get("cluster_breadth") or 0.0)
        d.sector_avg_return = float(row.get("cluster_avg_return") or 0.0)
        d.sector_member_count = int(float(row.get("cluster_members") or 0.0)) or None
        d.source_sector = "LIVE_POOL70"

        srow = {
            "stage1_score": float(row.get("integrated_score") or row.get("stage1_score") or 0.0),
            "stage1_state": str(row.get("stage1_state") or ""),
        }
        score, comp, reasons = engine.final_score(srow, d)
        grade = "S" if score >= 88 else "A" if score >= 80 else "B" if score >= 72 else "WATCH" if score >= 64 else "REJECT"
        payload = {
            "deep_score": round(float(score), 2),
            "deep_grade": grade,
            "deep_coverage": round(float(d.coverage()), 3),
            "trade_strength": d.trade_strength,
            "foreign_net": d.foreign_net,
            "institution_net": d.institution_net,
            "program_net": d.program_net,
            "deep_reasons": " | ".join(reasons),
            "verified_kst": now_kst_iso(),
        }
        payload.update(comp)
        LIVE_DEEP_CACHE[cache_key] = payload
        LIVE_DEEP_REFRESHED_AT[cache_key] = time.monotonic()
        with LOCK:
            STATE["live_deep_refresh_symbol"] = code
            STATE["live_deep_refresh_error"] = None
        logging.getLogger("alpha500_live_rank").info(
            "LIVE DEEP REFRESH symbol=%s score=%.1f grade=%s coverage=%.2f",
            code, score, grade, d.coverage(),
        )
        return True
    except Exception as e:
        LIVE_DEEP_REFRESHED_AT[cache_key] = time.monotonic()
        with LOCK:
            STATE["live_deep_refresh_symbol"] = code
            STATE["live_deep_refresh_error"] = f"{type(e).__name__}: {str(e)[:180]}"
        logging.getLogger("alpha500_live_rank").warning("LIVE DEEP REFRESH failed %s: %s", code, e)
        return False
    finally:
        LIVE_DEEP_REFRESH_LOCK.release()


def live_rank_loop():
    interval = max(5, int(os.getenv("LIVE_REBALANCE_SECONDS", "15")))
    deep_n = max(1, int(os.getenv("LIVE_DEEP_N", "30")))
    watch_n = max(1, int(os.getenv("LIVE_WATCH_N", "10")))
    require_deep = os.getenv("LIVE_WATCH_REQUIRE_DEEP", "true").strip().lower() in {"1", "true", "yes", "y", "on"}
    while True:
        try:
            summary = load_latest()
            info = market_session_now()
            if not isinstance(summary, dict) or not info.get("scan_allowed"):
                time.sleep(interval)
                continue
            if str(summary.get("venue") or "").upper() != str(info.get("venue") or "").upper():
                time.sleep(interval)
                continue

            _seed_live_deep_cache(summary)
            rows = _live_rows_with_quotes(summary)
            rows.sort(key=lambda r: float(r.get("live_score") or 0.0), reverse=True)
            live_deep = rows[:deep_n]

            ttl = max(60, int(os.getenv("LIVE_DEEP_TTL_SECONDS", "300")))
            refresh_candidate = next(
                (
                    r for r in live_deep
                    if not r.get("deep_verified")
                    or time.monotonic() - float(
                        LIVE_DEEP_REFRESHED_AT.get(
                            f"{str(summary.get('venue') or '').upper()}:{str(r.get('symbol', '')).zfill(6)}"
                        ) or 0.0
                    ) >= ttl
                ),
                None,
            )
            if refresh_candidate is not None:
                _deep_refresh_one(refresh_candidate, summary.get("venue"))
                rows = _live_rows_with_quotes(summary)
                rows.sort(key=lambda r: float(r.get("live_score") or 0.0), reverse=True)
                live_deep = rows[:deep_n]

            eligible_live = [r for r in live_deep if r.get("live_watch_eligible", True)]
            watch_source = [r for r in eligible_live if r.get("deep_verified")] if require_deep else eligible_live
            live_watch = []
            for r in watch_source[:watch_n]:
                z = dict(r)
                z["selection_type"] = "LIVE_WATCH"
                live_watch.append(z)

            stamp = now_kst_iso()
            with LIVE_RANK_LOCK:
                LIVE_RANK_SNAPSHOT["venue"] = str(summary.get("venue") or "").upper()
                LIVE_RANK_SNAPSHOT["live_deep30"] = live_deep
                LIVE_RANK_SNAPSHOT["live_watch10"] = live_watch
                LIVE_RANK_SNAPSHOT["live_rebalanced_kst"] = stamp
            with LOCK:
                STATE["live_pool_count"] = len(rows)
                STATE["live_deep_count"] = len(live_deep)
                STATE["live_watch_count"] = len(live_watch)
                STATE["live_rebalanced_kst"] = stamp

            logging.getLogger("alpha500_live_rank").info(
                "LIVE RANK pool=%d deep=%d watch=%d venue=%s",
                len(rows), len(live_deep), len(live_watch), summary.get("venue"),
            )
        except Exception as e:
            logging.getLogger("alpha500_live_rank").warning("LIVE RANK loop failed: %s", e)
        time.sleep(interval)



def _sector_norm(v, lo, hi):
    try:
        x = float(v)
    except Exception:
        return 0.5
    if hi <= lo:
        return 0.5
    return max(0.0, min(1.0, (x - lo) / (hi - lo)))


def _live_sector_rows(summary, qmap, index_returns, previous_rows, rep_n=5):
    rows = json.loads(json.dumps(summary.get("sector_live_pool") or [], ensure_ascii=False, default=str))
    venue = str(summary.get("venue") or "").upper()
    enriched = []
    for r in rows:
        code = str(r.get("symbol", "")).zfill(6)
        q = qmap.get(code) or {}
        px = _quote_price(q)
        pct = _quote_pct(q)
        if px is not None:
            r["current_price"] = float(px)
        if pct is not None:
            r["change_pct"] = float(pct)
        market = str(r.get("market") or "").upper()
        bench = float(index_returns.get(market, 0.0))
        r["market_rs"] = float(r.get("change_pct") or 0.0) - bench

        snap_turn = float(r.get("turnover_snapshot") or 0.0)
        seed_accel = float(r.get("turnover_accel") or 1.0)
        live_turn = 0.0
        for k in ("value", "volume_amt", "acml_tr_pbmn"):
            try:
                if q.get(k) not in (None, ""):
                    live_turn = float(str(q.get(k)).replace(",", ""))
                    if live_turn > 0:
                        break
            except Exception:
                pass
        if snap_turn > 0 and live_turn > 0:
            r["turnover_accel_live"] = max(0.0, seed_accel * live_turn / snap_turn)
        else:
            r["turnover_accel_live"] = max(0.0, seed_accel)
        enriched.append(r)

    groups = {}
    for r in enriched:
        cluster = str(r.get("ENERGY_CLUSTER") or "")
        if cluster:
            groups.setdefault(cluster, []).append(r)

    prev_map = {str(x.get("ENERGY_CLUSTER")): x for x in (previous_rows or []) if isinstance(x, dict)}
    calculated = []
    for cluster, g in groups.items():
        mrs = [float(x.get("market_rs") or 0.0) for x in g]
        rets = [float(x.get("change_pct") or 0.0) for x in g]
        turns = [float(x.get("turnover_accel_live") or 1.0) for x in g]
        holds = [1.0 if bool(x.get("stage1_above_vwap")) and bool(x.get("stage1_ema20_hold")) else 0.0 for x in g]
        mean_mrs = sum(mrs) / max(1, len(mrs))
        avg_ret = sum(rets) / max(1, len(rets))
        breadth = sum(1 for x in mrs if x > 0.0) / max(1, len(mrs))
        turns_sorted = sorted(turns)
        turn = turns_sorted[len(turns_sorted)//2] if turns_sorted else 1.0
        hold = sum(holds) / max(1, len(holds))

        cap_pairs = []
        for x in g:
            cap = float(x.get("market_cap_100m") or 0.0)
            if cap > 0:
                cap_pairs.append((max(1.0, cap) ** 0.5, float(x.get("market_rs") or 0.0)))
        if len(cap_pairs) >= 2:
            sw = sum(w for w, _ in cap_pairs)
            power_mrs = sum(w*v for w, v in cap_pairs) / sw if sw else mean_mrs
            cap_coverage = len(cap_pairs) / max(1, len(g))
        else:
            power_mrs = mean_mrs
            cap_coverage = 0.0

        rotation = 35*_sector_norm(mean_mrs, -1.5, 2.0) + 25*breadth + 20*_sector_norm(turn, 0.70, 1.80) + 20*hold
        power = 60*_sector_norm(power_mrs, -1.5, 2.0) + 20*breadth + 20*_sector_norm(turn, 0.70, 1.80)
        energy = max(0.0, min(100.0, 0.65*rotation + 0.35*power))
        if mean_mrs < -1.0 and breadth < 0.30:
            state = "S5"
        elif energy >= 82 and (avg_ret >= 4.5 or turn >= 2.20):
            state = "S4"
        elif energy >= 70:
            state = "S3"
        elif energy >= 58:
            state = "S2"
        elif energy >= 45:
            state = "S1"
        else:
            state = "S0"

        # 대표종목은 현재 섹터 기여도가 큰 순서: 등락/시장대비RS/거래대금가속 + 기존 구조점수.
        def rep_score(x):
            ch = float(x.get("change_pct") or 0.0)
            rs = float(x.get("market_rs") or 0.0)
            ta = float(x.get("turnover_accel_live") or 1.0)
            base = float(x.get("integrated_score") or 0.0)
            return 7.0*ch + 9.0*rs + 8.0*min(3.0, ta) + 0.15*base
        reps = []
        for x in sorted(g, key=rep_score, reverse=True)[:max(1, int(rep_n))]:
            reps.append({
                "symbol": str(x.get("symbol") or "").zfill(6),
                "name": str(x.get("name") or ""),
                "current_price": round(float(x.get("current_price") or 0.0), 2),
                "change_pct": round(float(x.get("change_pct") or 0.0), 3),
            })

        calculated.append({
            "ENERGY_CLUSTER": cluster,
            "cluster_state": state,
            "cluster_energy": round(energy, 2),
            "cluster_breadth": round(breadth, 4),
            "cluster_avg_return": round(avg_ret, 3),
            "cluster_market_rs": round(mean_mrs, 3),
            "cluster_turnover_accel": round(turn, 3),
            "cluster_hold_ratio": round(hold, 4),
            "cluster_power": round(power, 2),
            "cluster_rotation": round(rotation, 2),
            "cluster_members": len(g),
            "cap_coverage": round(cap_coverage, 3),
            "representatives": reps,
            "venue": venue,
        })

    calculated.sort(key=lambda x: (float(x.get("cluster_energy") or 0.0), float(x.get("cluster_rotation") or 0.0)), reverse=True)
    for i, r in enumerate(calculated, 1):
        r["rank"] = i
        prev = prev_map.get(str(r.get("ENERGY_CLUSTER"))) or {}
        prev_rank = prev.get("rank")
        prev_energy = prev.get("cluster_energy")
        r["previous_rank"] = prev_rank
        r["energy_delta"] = None if prev_energy is None else round(float(r["cluster_energy"]) - float(prev_energy or 0.0), 2)
        if prev_rank is None:
            r["rank_move"] = None
            r["movement"] = "NEW"
        else:
            move = int(float(prev_rank)) - i
            r["rank_move"] = move
            r["movement"] = f"↑{move}" if move > 0 else (f"↓{abs(move)}" if move < 0 else "→")
    return calculated


def live_sector_loop():
    interval = max(30, int(os.getenv("LIVE_SECTOR_INTERVAL_SECONDS", "60")))
    rep_n = max(1, min(5, int(os.getenv("LIVE_SECTOR_REP_N", "5"))))
    krx_enabled = os.getenv("LIVE_SECTOR_KRX", "true").strip().lower() in {"1","true","yes","y","on"}
    nxt_enabled = os.getenv("LIVE_SECTOR_NXT", "false").strip().lower() in {"1","true","yes","y","on"}
    log = logging.getLogger("alpha500_live_sector")
    while True:
        try:
            summary = load_latest()
            info = market_session_now()
            if not isinstance(summary, dict) or not info.get("scan_allowed"):
                time.sleep(interval); continue
            venue = str(summary.get("venue") or "").upper()
            if venue != str(info.get("venue") or "").upper():
                time.sleep(interval); continue
            if (venue == "KRX" and not krx_enabled) or (venue == "NXT" and not nxt_enabled):
                with LOCK:
                    STATE["live_sector_source"] = "10MIN_SNAPSHOT"
                    STATE["live_sector_error"] = None
                time.sleep(interval); continue
            # NXT 500종목은 t8450 개별호출이므로 기본 OFF. 사용자가 명시적으로 켠 경우에만 실행한다.
            if venue == "NXT" and not nxt_enabled:
                time.sleep(interval); continue
            with LOCK:
                scan_busy = bool(STATE.get("running"))
            if scan_busy:
                time.sleep(min(5, interval)); continue
            pool = summary.get("sector_live_pool") or []
            if not pool:
                time.sleep(interval); continue
            codes = [str(r.get("symbol", "")).zfill(6) for r in pool if str(r.get("symbol", "")).zfill(6).isdigit()]
            engine = get_engine()
            engine.fast_history.set_venue(venue, str(info.get("exchgubun") or ("N" if venue == "NXT" else "K")))
            qmap = engine.fast_history.multi_quotes(codes)

            index_returns = {}
            if venue == "KRX":
                for market in ("KOSPI", "KOSDAQ"):
                    try:
                        z = engine.index.current(market)
                        index_returns[market] = float(z.get("return_pct") or 0.0)
                    except Exception:
                        z = (summary.get("index") or {}).get(market) or {}
                        index_returns[market] = float(z.get("return_pct") or 0.0)
            else:
                # NXT는 별도 지수 실시간 TR이 없으므로 동일 venue의 직전 스캔 benchmark만 사용.
                for market in ("KOSPI", "KOSDAQ"):
                    z = (summary.get("index") or {}).get(market) or {}
                    index_returns[market] = float(z.get("return_pct") or 0.0)

            with LIVE_SECTOR_LOCK:
                previous = LIVE_SECTOR_SNAPSHOT.get("sector_energy") or summary.get("sector_energy") or []
            sector_rows = _live_sector_rows(summary, qmap, index_returns, previous, rep_n=rep_n)
            stamp = now_kst_iso()
            source = "LIVE:t8407_500" if venue == "KRX" else "LIVE:t8450_NXT"
            with LIVE_SECTOR_LOCK:
                LIVE_SECTOR_SNAPSHOT["venue"] = venue
                LIVE_SECTOR_SNAPSHOT["sector_energy"] = sector_rows[:30]
                LIVE_SECTOR_SNAPSHOT["top_clusters"] = sector_rows[:15]
                LIVE_SECTOR_SNAPSHOT["updated_kst"] = stamp
                LIVE_SECTOR_SNAPSHOT["source"] = source
            with LOCK:
                STATE["live_sector_updated_kst"] = stamp
                STATE["live_sector_count"] = len(sector_rows)
                STATE["live_sector_source"] = source
                STATE["live_sector_error"] = None
            log.info("LIVE SECTOR sectors=%d quotes=%d venue=%s source=%s", len(sector_rows), len(qmap), venue, source)
        except Exception as e:
            with LOCK:
                STATE["live_sector_error"] = f"{type(e).__name__}: {str(e)[:180]}"
            logging.getLogger("alpha500_live_sector").warning("LIVE SECTOR loop failed: %s", e)
        time.sleep(interval)


def live_quote_loop():
    """WebSocket 누락/장애 종목만 REST로 보완하는 fallback 루프."""
    while True:
        fallback_used = False
        try:
            summary = load_latest()
            info = market_session_now()
            if bool(info.get("scan_allowed")):
                venue = str(info.get("venue") or "KRX").upper()
                targets = _ws_target_map(summary, venue)
                stale = [c for c in targets if not (_quote_is_fresh(c) and _quote_source(c).startswith("WS:"))]
                if stale:
                    history = get_rest_history(venue)
                    qmap = history.multi_quotes(stale)
                    if market_session_now().get("venue") == venue:
                        for code, q in qmap.items():
                            # A tick received while REST was in flight takes precedence.
                            if not (_quote_is_fresh(code) and _quote_source(code).startswith("WS:")):
                                _set_live_quote(code, q, "REST:t8450" if venue == "NXT" else "REST:t8407", venue)
                                fallback_used = True
            _set_ws_state(STATE.get("ws_status", "WAITING"), rest_fallback=fallback_used)
        except Exception as e:
            logging.getLogger("alpha500_live").warning("REST fallback failed: %s", e)
            _set_ws_state(STATE.get("ws_status", "STALE"), rest_fallback=False)
        time.sleep(REST_FALLBACK_SECONDS)

def _preclose_path(venue):
    return RESULT_DIR / f"latest_preclose_{str(venue or 'KRX').upper()}.json"


def _nxt_eod_hold_path():
    return RESULT_DIR / "nxt_eod_hold.json"


def _read_nxt_eod_hold():
    global NXT_EOD_HOLD
    if isinstance(NXT_EOD_HOLD, dict):
        return NXT_EOD_HOLD
    try:
        z = json.loads(_nxt_eod_hold_path().read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        if isinstance(z, dict):
            NXT_EOD_HOLD = z
            return z
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def save_nxt_eod_hold():
    """마지막 NXT 판정을 익일 첫 정상 NXT 판정 전까지 HOLD한다.

    /data가 비영속인 Render Free 재시작까지는 보장할 수 없으므로 상태에 LOCAL_BEST_EFFORT를 명시한다.
    """
    global NXT_EOD_HOLD
    summary = load_latest_for_venue("NXT")
    if not isinstance(summary, dict):
        raise RuntimeError("NXT EOD summary unavailable")
    snap = {
        "captured_at_kst": now_kst_iso(),
        "trade_date": str(summary.get("timestamp_kst") or now_kst_iso())[:10],
        "venue": "NXT",
        "persistence": "LOCAL_BEST_EFFORT",
        "summary": summary,
        "close_bet_nxt": _read_close_bet("NXT"),
    }
    p = _nxt_eod_hold_path(); tmp = p.with_suffix(p.suffix + ".tmp")
    tmp.write_text(json.dumps(snap, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default=str), encoding="utf-8")
    tmp.replace(p)
    NXT_EOD_HOLD = snap
    with LOCK:
        STATE["nxt_eod_hold_at"] = snap["captured_at_kst"]
        STATE["nxt_eod_hold_trade_date"] = snap["trade_date"]
        STATE["nxt_eod_hold_status"] = snap["persistence"]
    logging.getLogger("alpha500_close_bet").info(
        "NXT EOD HOLD saved trade_date=%s source=%s", snap["trade_date"], summary.get("timestamp_kst")
    )
    return snap


def _preclose_score_row(r, cluster_avg):
    try: change = float(r.get("change_pct") or 0.0)
    except Exception: change = 0.0
    lo = float(os.getenv("CLOSE_BET_MIN_RETURN_PCT", "-3.0"))
    hi = float(os.getenv("CLOSE_BET_MAX_RETURN_PCT", "7.0"))
    if not (lo <= change <= hi):
        return None
    try: integ = float(r.get("integrated_score") or 0.0)
    except Exception: integ = 0.0
    try: energy = float(r.get("cluster_energy") or 0.0)
    except Exception: energy = 0.0
    try: base_mrs = float(r.get("market_rs") or 0.0)
    except Exception: base_mrs = 0.0
    cluster = str(r.get("ENERGY_CLUSTER") or "")
    rel = change - float(cluster_avg.get(cluster, 0.0))
    score = 0.50 * integ + 0.25 * energy + 8.0 * max(-2.0, min(2.0, rel)) + 4.0 * max(-2.0, min(2.0, base_mrs))
    if change > 5.5:
        score -= (change - 5.5) * 5.0
    if bool(r.get("stage1_above_vwap", False)): score += 3.0
    if bool(r.get("stage1_ema20_hold", False)): score += 2.0
    return round(score, 2)


def build_preclose_rescan(venue, trigger_label):
    """15:20/19:20에 기존 500 sector_live_pool을 최신 가격으로 재조회해 신규 강자 누락을 줄인다."""
    venue = str(venue or "KRX").upper()
    if not PRECLOSE_LOCK.acquire(blocking=False):
        return PRECLOSE_SNAPSHOT.get(venue)
    try:
        summary = load_latest_for_venue(venue)
        if not isinstance(summary, dict):
            raise RuntimeError(f"{venue} latest summary unavailable for preclose")
        rows = [dict(x) for x in (summary.get("sector_live_pool") or []) if isinstance(x, dict)]
        if not rows:
            rows = _candidate_union(summary)
        codes = [str(r.get("symbol", "")).zfill(6) for r in rows]
        qmap = get_rest_history(venue).multi_quotes(codes) if codes else {}
        refreshed = []
        for r in rows:
            code = str(r.get("symbol", "")).zfill(6)
            q = qmap.get(code) or {}
            px = _quote_price(q); pct = _quote_pct(q)
            if px is not None: r["current_price"] = px
            if pct is not None: r["change_pct"] = pct
            r["close_bet_quote_source"] = "REST:t8450" if venue == "NXT" else "REST:t8407"
            refreshed.append(r)
        cluster_vals = {}
        for r in refreshed:
            c = str(r.get("ENERGY_CLUSTER") or "")
            try: v = float(r.get("change_pct"))
            except Exception: continue
            cluster_vals.setdefault(c, []).append(v)
        cluster_avg = {c: (sum(v)/len(v) if v else 0.0) for c,v in cluster_vals.items()}
        scored = []
        for r in refreshed:
            sc = _preclose_score_row(r, cluster_avg)
            if sc is None: continue
            r["preclose_score"] = sc
            r["selection_type"] = f"{venue}_PRECLOSE_RESCAN"
            scored.append(r)
        scored.sort(key=lambda x: float(x.get("preclose_score") or 0.0), reverse=True)
        top_n = max(20, int(os.getenv("PRECLOSE_RESCAN_TOP_N", "60")))
        snap = {
            "captured_at_kst": now_kst_iso(), "venue": venue, "trigger": trigger_label,
            "universe_checked": len(rows), "priced_count": len(qmap), "candidate_count": min(len(scored), top_n),
            "data_status": "REST_REFRESH", "candidates": scored[:top_n],
        }
        p = _preclose_path(venue); tmp = p.with_suffix(p.suffix + ".tmp")
        tmp.write_text(json.dumps(snap, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default=str), encoding="utf-8"); tmp.replace(p)
        PRECLOSE_SNAPSHOT[venue] = snap
        with LOCK:
            STATE[f"preclose_{venue.lower()}_at"] = snap["captured_at_kst"]
            STATE[f"preclose_{venue.lower()}_count"] = snap["candidate_count"]
            STATE["preclose_last_error"] = None
        logging.getLogger("alpha500_close_bet").info(
            "PRECLOSE RESCAN venue=%s checked=%d priced=%d candidates=%d",
            venue, len(rows), len(qmap), snap["candidate_count"]
        )
        return snap
    finally:
        PRECLOSE_LOCK.release()


def _read_preclose(venue):
    venue = str(venue or "KRX").upper()
    z = PRECLOSE_SNAPSHOT.get(venue)
    if isinstance(z, dict): return z
    try:
        z = json.loads(_preclose_path(venue).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        if isinstance(z, dict):
            PRECLOSE_SNAPSHOT[venue] = z
            return z
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def _close_bet_path(venue, latest=True, ymd=None):
    venue = str(venue or "KRX").upper()
    if latest:
        return RESULT_DIR / f"latest_close_bet_{venue}.json"
    return RESULT_DIR / f"close_bet_{venue}_{ymd or now_kst().strftime('%Y%m%d')}.json"


def _read_close_bet(venue):
    try:
        return json.loads(_close_bet_path(venue).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except Exception:
        return None


def _candidate_union(summary):
    if not isinstance(summary, dict):
        return []
    seen, rows = set(), []
    for key in ("top_final", "live_watch10", "live_deep30", "top_watch", "live_pool"):
        for r in summary.get(key) or []:
            code = str(r.get("symbol", "")).zfill(6)
            if not (code.isdigit() and len(code) == 6) or code in seen:
                continue
            seen.add(code); rows.append(dict(r))
    return rows


def _close_bet_refresh(rows, venue):
    venue = str(venue or "KRX").upper()
    rows = [dict(r) for r in rows[:40]]
    codes = [str(r.get("symbol", "")).zfill(6) for r in rows]
    rest_map = {}
    try:
        if codes:
            rest_map = get_rest_history(venue).multi_quotes(codes)
    except Exception as e:
        logging.getLogger("alpha500_close_bet").warning("CLOSE BET REST refresh failed venue=%s: %s", venue, e)
    with LIVE_LOCK:
        live_map = dict(LIVE_QUOTES); live_meta = {k: dict(v) for k, v in LIVE_QUOTE_META.items()}
    for r in rows:
        code = str(r.get("symbol", "")).zfill(6); q = None; src = "SNAPSHOT"
        m = live_meta.get(code) or {}
        if str(m.get("venue") or "").upper() == venue and _quote_is_fresh(code, max_age=max(10, WS_STALE_SECONDS)) and str(m.get("source") or "").startswith("WS:"):
            q = live_map.get(code); src = str(m.get("source") or "WS")
        elif code in rest_map:
            q = rest_map.get(code); src = "REST:t8450" if venue == "NXT" else "REST:t8407"
        if isinstance(q, dict):
            px = _quote_price(q); pct = _quote_pct(q)
            if px is not None: r["current_price"] = px
            if pct is not None: r["change_pct"] = pct
        r["close_bet_quote_source"] = src
    return rows


def _close_bet_score(r, venue, krx_close_map=None):
    try: change = float(r.get("change_pct") or 0.0)
    except Exception: change = 0.0
    if not (float(os.getenv("CLOSE_BET_MIN_RETURN_PCT", "-3.0")) <= change <= float(os.getenv("CLOSE_BET_MAX_RETURN_PCT", "7.0"))):
        return None
    state, asym = str(r.get("cluster_state") or ""), str(r.get("asym_rs_class") or "")
    if state not in {"S2", "S3"} and asym not in {"SUPER", "STRONG"}: return None
    stage = str(r.get("stage1_state") or "")
    if stage and stage not in {"UP_START", "REVERSAL_ARMED", "DOWN_WEAKEN", "UP_TREND"}: return None
    deep=float(r.get("deep_score") or 0.0); integ=float(r.get("integrated_score") or r.get("stage1_score") or 0.0)
    energy=float(r.get("cluster_energy") or 0.0); mrs=float(r.get("market_rs_score") or 50.0); strength=float(r.get("trade_strength") or 100.0)
    grade=str(r.get("deep_grade") or ""); grade_bonus={"S":8.0,"A":6.0,"B":3.5,"WATCH":0.0}.get(grade,-3.0)
    score=0.38*deep+0.25*integ+0.17*energy+0.10*mrs+0.10*min(100.0,max(0.0,strength-60.0))+grade_bonus
    if change>5.5: score-=(change-5.5)*4.0
    if state=="S3": score+=3.0
    if bool(r.get("deep_verified",False)): score+=2.0
    premium=None
    if str(venue).upper()=="NXT" and krx_close_map:
        ref=krx_close_map.get(str(r.get("symbol","")).zfill(6))
        try:
            ref_px=float((ref or {}).get("current_price") or 0.0); cur_px=float(r.get("current_price") or 0.0)
            if ref_px>0 and cur_px>0:
                premium=(cur_px/ref_px-1.0)*100.0
                if premium>2.0: score-=min(8.0,(premium-2.0)*2.5)
        except Exception: premium=None
    reasons=[]
    if state in {"S2","S3"}: reasons.append(state)
    if deep>=72: reasons.append(f"DEEP {deep:.1f}")
    if integ>=60: reasons.append(f"통합 {integ:.1f}")
    if strength>=120: reasons.append(f"체결강도 {strength:.0f}")
    if asym in {"SUPER","STRONG"}: reasons.append(f"비대칭RS {asym}")
    return round(score,2), premium, reasons


def build_close_bet(venue, trigger_label):
    venue=str(venue or "KRX").upper(); summary=load_latest_for_venue(venue)
    if not isinstance(summary,dict): raise RuntimeError(f"{venue} latest summary unavailable")
    base_rows=_candidate_union(summary)
    pre=_read_preclose(venue) or {}
    merged={str(r.get("symbol","")).zfill(6):dict(r) for r in base_rows if isinstance(r,dict)}
    for r in pre.get("candidates") or []:
        if not isinstance(r,dict): continue
        code=str(r.get("symbol","")).zfill(6)
        if code in merged:
            x=dict(r); x.update({k:v for k,v in merged[code].items() if k not in x or x.get(k) in (None,"")}); merged[code]=x
        else:
            merged[code]=dict(r)
    rows=_close_bet_refresh(list(merged.values()),venue)
    krx_close=_read_close_bet("KRX") if venue=="NXT" else None
    krx_close_map={str(x.get("symbol","")).zfill(6):x for x in ((krx_close or {}).get("candidates") or [])}
    scored=[]
    for r in rows:
        z=_close_bet_score(r,venue,krx_close_map)
        if z is None: continue
        score,premium,reasons=z; o=dict(r); o["close_bet_score"]=score; o["nxt_vs_krx_close_pct"]=None if premium is None else round(premium,3); o["close_bet_reasons"]=reasons; o["selection_type"]=f"{venue}_CLOSE_BET"; scored.append(o)
    scored.sort(key=lambda x:(float(x.get("close_bet_score") or 0.0),float(x.get("deep_score") or 0.0)),reverse=True)
    candidates=scored[:max(1,int(os.getenv("CLOSE_BET_TOP_N","5")))]
    sources=sorted({str(x.get("close_bet_quote_source") or "SNAPSHOT") for x in candidates})
    data_status="WS_CONFIRMED" if candidates and all(x.startswith("WS:") for x in sources) else ("REST_FALLBACK" if any(x.startswith("REST:") for x in sources) else "SNAPSHOT")
    snap={"captured_at_kst":now_kst_iso(),"trade_date":now_kst().strftime("%Y-%m-%d"),"venue":venue,"trigger":trigger_label,"source_summary_kst":summary.get("timestamp_kst"),"preclose_rescan_kst":(pre or {}).get("captured_at_kst"),"preclose_universe_checked":int((pre or {}).get("universe_checked") or 0),"candidate_count":len(candidates),"data_status":data_status,"quote_sources":sources,"no_force_fill":True,"candidates":candidates}
    for p in (_close_bet_path(venue),_close_bet_path(venue,latest=False,ymd=now_kst().strftime("%Y%m%d"))):
        tmp=p.with_suffix(p.suffix+".tmp"); tmp.write_text(json.dumps(snap,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2,default=str),encoding="utf-8"); tmp.replace(p)
    with LOCK:
        if venue=="KRX": STATE["close_bet_krx_at"]=snap["captured_at_kst"]; STATE["close_bet_krx_count"]=len(candidates)
        else: STATE["close_bet_nxt_at"]=snap["captured_at_kst"]; STATE["close_bet_nxt_count"]=len(candidates)
        STATE["close_bet_last_error"]=None
    logging.getLogger("alpha500_close_bet").info("CLOSE BET venue=%s trigger=%s candidates=%d data_status=%s sources=%s",venue,trigger_label,len(candidates),data_status,sources)
    return snap


def close_bet_loop():
    poll=max(5,int(os.getenv("CLOSE_BET_POLL_SECONDS","10")))
    done={"PRECLOSE_KRX":None,"KRX":None,"PRECLOSE_NXT":None,"NXT":None,"EOD_NXT":None}
    while True:
        try:
            if os.getenv("CLOSE_BET_ENABLED","true").strip().lower() not in {"1","true","yes","y","on"}: time.sleep(poll); continue
            n=now_kst(); day=n.strftime("%Y-%m-%d"); hm=n.hour*60+n.minute
            pre_enabled=os.getenv("PRECLOSE_RESCAN_ENABLED","true").strip().lower() in {"1","true","yes","y","on"}
            if pre_enabled and 15*60+20<=hm<15*60+30 and done["PRECLOSE_KRX"]!=day:
                build_preclose_rescan("KRX","15:20_KRX_PRECLOSE"); done["PRECLOSE_KRX"]=day
            if 15*60+30<=hm<15*60+40 and done["KRX"]!=day:
                build_close_bet("KRX","15:30_KRX_CLOSE"); done["KRX"]=day
            if pre_enabled and 19*60+20<=hm<19*60+30 and done["PRECLOSE_NXT"]!=day:
                build_preclose_rescan("NXT","19:20_NXT_PRECLOSE"); done["PRECLOSE_NXT"]=day
            if 19*60+30<=hm<20*60 and done["NXT"]!=day:
                build_close_bet("NXT","19:30_NXT_CLOSE_BET"); done["NXT"]=day
            eod_enabled=os.getenv("NXT_EOD_HOLD_ENABLED","true").strip().lower() in {"1","true","yes","y","on"}
            eod_min=int(os.getenv("NXT_EOD_HOLD_MINUTE","1195"))
            if eod_enabled and eod_min<=hm<20*60 and done["EOD_NXT"]!=day:
                save_nxt_eod_hold(); done["EOD_NXT"]=day
        except Exception as e:
            with LOCK:
                STATE["close_bet_last_error"]=f"{type(e).__name__}: {str(e)[:220]}"
                STATE["preclose_last_error"]=STATE["close_bet_last_error"]
            logging.getLogger("alpha500_close_bet").warning("CLOSE BET/PRECLOSE loop failed: %s",e)
        time.sleep(poll)


def _attach_close_bet(result):
    if not isinstance(result,dict): result={}
    out=json.loads(json.dumps(result,ensure_ascii=False,default=str)); out["close_bet_krx"]=_read_close_bet("KRX"); out["close_bet_nxt"]=_read_close_bet("NXT"); return out


def run_scan(session_info=None):
    info = dict(session_info or market_session_now())
    if not bool(info.get("scan_allowed")):
        with LOCK:
            STATE["venue"] = info.get("venue", "NXT")
            STATE["session"] = info.get("session", "WAITING_NXT")
            if not STATE["running"]:
                STATE["status"] = "WAITING"
                STATE["phase"] = info.get("session", "WAITING")
                STATE["progress_current"] = 0
                STATE["progress_total"] = 0
                STATE["progress_message"] = "다음 거래 세션 대기 중"
        return False
    with LOCK:
        if STATE["running"]:
            return False
        STATE["running"] = True
        STATE["venue"] = info.get("venue", "KRX")
        STATE["session"] = info.get("session", "KRX_REGULAR")
        STATE["status"] = "SCANNING"
        STATE["last_started"] = now_kst_iso()
        STATE["last_error"] = None
        STATE["phase"] = "UNIVERSE"
        STATE["progress_current"] = 0
        STATE["progress_total"] = 500
        STATE["progress_message"] = f"{STATE['session']} · {STATE['venue']} 유니버스 구성 중"
    try:
        engine = get_engine()
        *_, final, summary = engine.run_integrated(bootstrap_missing=True, session_info=info)
        with LOCK:
            STATE["last_summary"] = summary
            STATE["venue"] = summary.get("venue", STATE["venue"])
            STATE["session"] = summary.get("session", STATE["session"])
            STATE["status"] = "READY"
            STATE["last_finished"] = summary.get("timestamp_kst") or now_kst_iso()
            STATE["phase"] = "COMPLETE"
            STATE["progress_current"] = 1
            STATE["progress_total"] = 1
            STATE["progress_message"] = f"{STATE['session']} 완료 · FINAL {int(summary.get('final_count',0))} · WATCH {len(summary.get('top_watch') or [])}"
    except Exception as e:
        with LOCK:
            STATE["status"] = "ERROR"
            STATE["last_error"] = f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"
            STATE["last_finished"] = now_kst_iso()
            STATE["phase"] = "ERROR"
            STATE["progress_message"] = STATE["last_error"]
        traceback.print_exc()
    finally:
        with LOCK:
            STATE["running"] = False
    return True


def scan_loop():
    initial = int(os.getenv("INITIAL_SCAN_DELAY_SECONDS", "3"))
    interval = int(os.getenv("SCAN_INTERVAL_SECONDS", "600"))
    poll = int(os.getenv("SESSION_POLL_SECONDS", "15"))
    logging.getLogger("alpha500_scheduler").info(
        "scheduler interval=%ss poll=%ss non_overlap=true history_gap_days=%s",
        interval, poll, os.getenv("HISTORY_REFRESH_GAP_DAYS", "4")
    )
    time.sleep(max(0, initial))
    last_session = None
    last_run = 0.0
    while True:
        info = market_session_now()
        session_key = str(info.get("session"))
        with LOCK:
            STATE["venue"] = info.get("venue", "NXT")
            STATE["session"] = session_key
            busy = STATE["running"]
            if not info.get("scan_allowed") and not busy:
                STATE["status"] = "WAITING" if STATE.get("last_finished") is None else "READY"
                STATE["phase"] = session_key
                STATE["progress_message"] = "NXT 08:00 개장 대기" if session_key == "WAITING_NXT" else "세션 전환 대기"
        due = bool(info.get("scan_allowed")) and (session_key != last_session or time.monotonic() - last_run >= max(60, interval))
        if due and not busy:
            if run_scan(info):
                last_run = time.monotonic()
                last_session = session_key
        time.sleep(max(5, poll))


def state_copy():
    with LOCK:
        d = dict(STATE)
    if d.get("ws_status") == "RECEIVING" and d.get("ws_last_message_kst"):
        age = (now_kst() - datetime.fromisoformat(d["ws_last_message_kst"])).total_seconds()
        venue_for_stale = str(d.get("venue") or market_session_now().get("venue") or "KRX").upper()
        stale_limit = (max(WS_STALE_SECONDS, int(os.getenv("NXT_WS_STALE_SECONDS", "45")))
                       if venue_for_stale == "NXT" else WS_STALE_SECONDS)
        d["ws_tick_age_seconds"] = round(age, 1)
        d["ws_stale_limit_seconds"] = stale_limit
        if age > stale_limit:
            d["ws_status"] = "STALE"
    d["ls_configured"] = bool(os.getenv("LS_APP_KEY") and os.getenv("LS_APP_SECRET"))
    d["master"] = MASTER
    d["scan_interval_seconds"] = int(os.getenv("SCAN_INTERVAL_SECONDS", "600"))
    d["live_rebalance_seconds"] = int(os.getenv("LIVE_REBALANCE_SECONDS", "15"))
    d["live_pool_max"] = int(os.getenv("LIVE_POOL_MAX", "70"))
    d["live_sector_interval_seconds"] = int(os.getenv("LIVE_SECTOR_INTERVAL_SECONDS", "60"))
    d["close_bet_enabled"] = os.getenv("CLOSE_BET_ENABLED", "true").strip().lower() in {"1","true","yes","y","on"}
    d["close_bet_top_n"] = int(os.getenv("CLOSE_BET_TOP_N", "5"))
    d["preclose_rescan_enabled"] = os.getenv("PRECLOSE_RESCAN_ENABLED", "true").strip().lower() in {"1","true","yes","y","on"}
    d["nxt_eod_hold_enabled"] = os.getenv("NXT_EOD_HOLD_ENABLED", "true").strip().lower() in {"1","true","yes","y","on"}
    d["nxt_eod_persistence"] = "LOCAL_BEST_EFFORT"
    d["nxt_eod_persistence_warning"] = "Render ephemeral filesystem restart/spin-down may remove local HOLD unless persistent storage is configured"
    d["now_kst"] = now_kst_iso()
    live = market_session_now()
    d["expected_venue"] = live.get("venue")
    d["expected_session"] = live.get("session")
    total = int(d.get("progress_total") or 0)
    cur = int(d.get("progress_current") or 0)
    d["progress_pct"] = round(cur / total * 100, 1) if total > 0 else 0.0
    d["scan_interval_seconds"] = int(os.getenv("SCAN_INTERVAL_SECONDS", "600"))
    d["history_refresh_gap_days"] = int(os.getenv("HISTORY_REFRESH_GAP_DAYS", "4"))
    d["ls_tr_retries"] = int(os.getenv("LS_TR_RETRIES", "3"))
    return d


class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    def _json(self, status, obj):
        body = json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default=str).encode("utf-8")
        self.send_response(status)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8")
        self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store, no-cache, must-revalidate")
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
        self.end_headers()
        if self.command != "HEAD":
            self.wfile.write(body)

    def _html(self, status, text):
        body = text.encode("utf-8")
        self.send_response(status)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", "text/html; charset=utf-8")
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
        self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store, no-cache, must-revalidate")
        self.end_headers()
        if self.command != "HEAD":
            self.wfile.write(body)

<script>
const esc=v=>String(v??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,m=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[m]));
const num=(v,d=0)=>{const n=Number(v);return Number.isFinite(n)?n.toLocaleString('ko-KR',{minimumFractionDigits:d,maximumFractionDigits:d}):'-'};
const pct=v=>{const n=Number(v);return Number.isFinite(n)?((n>0?'+':'')+n.toFixed(2)+'%'):'-'};
const cls=v=>Number(v)>0?'pos':Number(v)<0?'neg':'';
const box=(k,v)=>`<div class="sbox"><div class="k">${esc(k)}</div><div class="snum">${esc(v)}</div></div>`;
function mergeHistory(serverRows,venue){const key='ALPHA500_HISTORY_'+String(venue||'KRX').toUpperCase();let local=[];try{local=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)||'[]');if(!Array.isArray(local))local=[]}catch(e){local=[]}const m=new Map();[...(serverRows||[]),...local].forEach(r=>{const k=[r.selected_at_kst||'',r.symbol||'',r.selection_type||'',r.entry_price||''].join('|');if(!m.has(k))m.set(k,r)});const out=[...m.values()].sort((a,b)=>String(b.selected_at_kst||'').localeCompare(String(a.selected_at_kst||''))).slice(0,200);try{localStorage.setItem(key,JSON.stringify(out))}catch(e){}return out;}
function clamp(v,a,b){v=Number(v)||0;return Math.max(a,Math.min(b,v))}
function liveRankScore(r){
 const srv=Number(r.live_score);
 if(Number.isFinite(srv)&&srv>0)return srv;
 const base=0.50*(Number(r.integrated_score)||0)+0.20*(Number(r.deep_score)||0)+0.15*(Number(r.cluster_energy)||0);
 const cp=Number(r.current_price)||0, ep=Number(r.entry_price)||0, ch=Number(r.change_pct)||0;
 const er=(cp>0&&ep>0)?((cp/ep-1)*100):0;
 let dyn=50+5*clamp(er,-5,5)+2*clamp(ch,-5,5);
 if(ch>5) dyn-=8*(ch-5);
 if(er<-4) dyn-=8;
 return base+0.15*dyn;
}
function candidateRows(rows){
 if(!rows?.length)return '<tr><td colspan="12" class="empty left">현재 표시할 후보가 없습니다.</td></tr>';
 const sorted=[...rows].sort((a,b)=>liveRankScore(b)-liveRankScore(a));
 return sorted.map((r,i)=>`<tr><td>${i+1}</td><td class="left"><b>${esc(r.name)}</b></td><td>${num(r.current_price)}</td><td class="${cls(r.change_pct)}">${pct(r.change_pct)}</td><td>${num(r.entry_price)}</td><td>${num(r.integrated_score,1)}</td><td>${num(r.deep_score,1)}</td><td><span class="badge">${esc(r.deep_grade)}</span></td><td class="left">${esc(r.MEGA_SECTOR)}</td><td class="left">${esc(r.ENERGY_CLUSTER)}</td><td>${num(r.cluster_energy,1)}</td><td>${esc(r.cluster_state)}</td></tr>`).join('')
}
function sectorRows(rows){
 if(!rows?.length)return '<tr><td colspan="11" class="empty left">섹터 에너지 데이터 준비 중</td></tr>';
 return rows.slice(0,30).map(r=>{
  let mv=esc(r.movement),mc=String(r.movement||'').startsWith('↑')?'up':String(r.movement||'').startsWith('↓')?'down':'';
  const reps=(r.representatives||[]).slice(0,5).map(x=>`<span class="sectorRepItem"><span class="sectorRepName">${esc(x.name)}</span><span class="sectorRepPrice">${num(x.current_price)}</span><span class="${cls(x.change_pct)}">${pct(x.change_pct)}</span></span>`).join('');
  const main=`<tr class="sectorMain"><td>${num(r.rank)}</td><td class="left"><b>${esc(r.ENERGY_CLUSTER)}</b></td><td>${esc(r.cluster_state)}</td><td>${num(r.cluster_energy,1)}</td><td class="${cls(r.energy_delta)}">${r.energy_delta==null?'-':(Number(r.energy_delta)>0?'+':'')+num(r.energy_delta,1)}</td><td class="${mc}">${mv}</td><td>${pct(Number(r.cluster_breadth)*100)}</td><td class="${cls(r.cluster_avg_return)}">${pct(r.cluster_avg_return)}</td><td class="${cls(r.cluster_market_rs)}">${num(r.cluster_market_rs,2)}</td><td>${num(r.cluster_turnover_accel,2)}</td><td>${num(r.cluster_members)}</td></tr>`;
  const sub=`<tr class="sectorReps"><td></td><td colspan="10" class="left"><div class="sectorRepList">${reps||'<span>대표종목 계산 중</span>'}</div></td></tr>`;
  return main+sub;
 }).join('')
}
function closeBetRows(snap,isNxt){const rows=snap?.candidates||[];if(!rows.length)return '<tr><td colspan="10" class="empty left">적격 종가배팅 후보 없음</td></tr>';return rows.map((r,i)=>`<tr><td>${i+1}</td><td class="left"><b>${esc(r.name)}</b></td><td>${num(r.current_price)}</td><td class="${cls(r.change_pct)}">${pct(r.change_pct)}</td><td>${num(r.close_bet_score,1)}</td><td>${num(r.deep_score,1)}</td><td>${num(r.cluster_energy,1)}</td><td>${isNxt?(r.nxt_vs_krx_close_pct==null?'-':pct(r.nxt_vs_krx_close_pct)):num(r.trade_strength,0)}</td><td>${esc(r.close_bet_quote_source||snap.data_status||'-')}</td><td class="left">${esc((r.close_bet_reasons||[]).join(' · '))}</td></tr>`).join('')}
function historyRows(rows){if(!rows?.length)return '<tr><td colspan="14" class="empty left">아직 선정 히스토리가 없습니다.</td></tr>';return rows.slice(0,200).map(r=>`<tr><td class="left">${esc(r.selected_at_kst||'-')}</td><td>${esc(r.venue||'-')}</td><td>${esc(r.session||'-')}</td><td>${num(r.occurrence)}</td><td>${esc(r.selection_type)}</td><td class="left"><b>${esc(r.name)}</b></td><td>${num(r.current_price)}</td><td class="${cls(r.change_pct)}">${pct(r.change_pct)}</td><td>${num(r.entry_price)}</td><td>${num(r.deep_score,1)}</td><td>${esc(r.deep_grade)}</td><td class="left">${esc(r.ENERGY_CLUSTER)}</td><td>${num(r.cluster_energy,1)}</td><td>${esc(r.cluster_state)}</td></tr>`).join('')}
async function refresh(){
 try{
  const s=await fetch('/api/status?x='+Date.now(),{cache:'no-store'}).then(r=>r.json());
  document.getElementById('version').textContent=s.version||'-';
  const st=document.getElementById('status');st.textContent=s.status+(s.running?' · RUNNING':'');st.className='v '+(s.status==='ERROR'?'bad':s.status==='READY'?'ok':'warn');

    def do_HEAD(self):
        path = urlparse(self.path).path
        if path in {"/", "/health", "/api/status", "/api/results"}:
            if path == "/":
                return self._dashboard()
            self.send_response(200); self.send_header("Content-Length", "0"); self.end_headers(); return
        self.send_response(404); self.end_headers()

    def do_GET(self):
        path = urlparse(self.path).path
        if path == "/":
            return self._dashboard()
        if path == "/health":
            s = state_copy()
            return self._json(200, {"ok": True, "version": VERSION, "provider": "LS", "venue": s.get("venue"), "session": s.get("session"), "status": s["status"], "running": s["running"], "ls_configured": s["ls_configured"], "now_kst": s["now_kst"]})
        if path == "/api/status":
            return self._json(200, state_copy())
        if path == "/api/results":
            latest = load_latest()
            payload = _apply_live_overlay(latest) if latest is not None else {"status": "NO_RESULT_YET", **state_copy()}
            return self._json(200, _attach_close_bet(payload))
        if path == "/api/run":
            with LOCK: busy = STATE["running"]
            ok = False
            if not busy:
                threading.Thread(target=run_scan, daemon=True).start(); ok = True
            return self._json(202 if ok else 409, {"accepted": ok, "state": state_copy()})
        return self._json(404, {"error": "not found"})

    def do_POST(self):
        if urlparse(self.path).path == "/api/run":
            return self.do_GET()
        return self._json(404, {"error": "not found"})

    def log_message(self, fmt, *args):
        print(f"WEB {self.address_string()} {fmt % args}")


def main():
    latest = load_latest()
    with LOCK:
        if latest:
            STATE["status"] = "READY"
            STATE["last_summary"] = latest
            STATE["last_finished"] = latest.get("timestamp_kst")
            STATE["venue"] = latest.get("venue", STATE.get("venue"))
            STATE["session"] = latest.get("session", STATE.get("session"))
            STATE["phase"] = "COMPLETE"
            STATE["progress_current"] = 1
            STATE["progress_total"] = 1
            STATE["progress_message"] = "직전 판정 결과 표시 중"
        else:
            STATE["status"] = "IDLE"
            STATE["phase"] = "IDLE"
            STATE["progress_message"] = "첫 판정 대기"
    if os.getenv("AUTO_SCAN", "true").strip().lower() in {"1", "true", "yes", "y", "on"}:
        threading.Thread(target=scan_loop, daemon=True).start()
    threading.Thread(target=live_ws_loop, daemon=True).start()
    threading.Thread(target=live_quote_loop, daemon=True).start()
    threading.Thread(target=live_rank_loop, daemon=True).start()
    threading.Thread(target=live_sector_loop, daemon=True).start()
    threading.Thread(target=close_bet_loop, daemon=True).start()
    server = ThreadingHTTPServer(("0.0.0.0", PORT), Handler)
    print(json.dumps({"event": "server_listening", "version": VERSION, "port": PORT, "kst": now_kst_iso()}, ensure_ascii=False))
    server.serve_forever()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
전체시장_2단계_딥스캔_V1_1.py

V1.1 핵심
=========
[1차] KOSPI/KOSDAQ 전체시장 가격구조 전수검색
      - 거래대금 상위 랭킹을 출발점으로 사용하지 않음
      - Alpha Heikin-Ashi / Fast HA / VWAP / EMA / RSI / RVOL / 압축 구조
      - Stage1 점수로 30~50개만 압축

[2차] 압축 후보에만 Deep Data 호출
      - 체결강도
      - 외국인/기관
      - 프로그램
      - 섹터 breadth
      - 실제 데이터 커버리지/신선도
      - 최종 Deep Score 재계산

데이터 진실성 원칙
=================
- KRX 전용. NXT/통합 데이터와 혼합하지 않음.
- OHLCV 봉은 한 종목/한 판정에서 단일 가격 공급자로 완결.
- Deep Feature는 서로 다른 증권사에서 받을 수 있으나,
  각 feature의 source를 별도 기록하며 venue는 KRX만 허용.
- KIS 종목별 투자자 데이터는 장중 실시간으로 간주하지 않음.
- LS가 연결되어 있으면 장중 Deep Data는 LS를 우선 사용.
- 누락 데이터를 0으로 위장하지 않고 coverage를 낮춰 감점.
- 섹터 breadth는 KIS 마스터의 업종분류 + 전체시장 당일 등락을
  이용하여 계산. 외부 sector API가 없어도 전체시장으로 직접 계산.

환경변수
========
DEEP_CANDIDATES=40

LS_KRX_EXCHGUBUN=K
SECTOR_MIN_MEMBERS=4

기존 V1.0 환경변수(KIS_APP_KEY, LS_APP_KEY 등)는 그대로 사용.
